| FORMAL_OR_THEORETICAL_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| MEASUREMENT_SCIENCE_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| EMPIRICAL_CAUSAL_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| EMPIRICAL_ASSOCIATIONAL_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| DOMAIN_CASE_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| REPLICATION_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY | HIGH |
| CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH | LOW |

**VARIATION_CONTROL_PROFILE**

- **DECISION_PROBLEM:** Decide whether an apparent failure warrants candidate correction or evaluator recalibration.
- **ASSUMED_DATA_GENERATING_PROCESS:** Candidate state and evaluator state both potentially vary; reference bridge observes both versions.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Frozen versions, protected reference cases and independent adjudication.
- **SIGNAL_OR_DISCRIMINATOR:** Reference-based evidence that old evaluator is biased/invalid and new one improves construct fidelity.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_COST:** Freezing a defective evaluator or false recalibration alarm.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_COST:** Outcome-contingent evaluator mutation that converts failure to pass.
- **NONSTATIONARITY_RISK:** Context/construct change may legitimately require revision.
- **INTERVENTION_RISK:** Core risk: threshold/answer movement after seeing failure.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Fix an authoritative clerical/specification error with documented source.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Independent prospective bridge, versioning and retrospective impact review.

**EXPERIMENT_DISCRIMINATION_PROFILE**

- **APPLICABILITY:** NOT_PRIMARY_FOR_THIS_PROPERTY

**QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT**

- Was the evaluator/threshold change proposed only after a candidate failed?
- What evidence independent of that candidate shows a measurement or construct defect?
- Is there a blinded old/new bridge and an assessment of affected prior conclusions?

### P053 — Separate candidate/process variation from evaluator/measurement variation before assigning corrective action

- **PROPERTY_ID:** `P053`
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Separate candidate/process variation from evaluator/measurement variation before assigning corrective action
- **PROPERTY_FAMILY:** `PROXY_EVALUATOR_AND_SYSTEM_GOVERNANCE`
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Measurement-system analysis, method agreement, measurement-error effects on control/experiments and evaluator drift governance.
- **LINEAGE_CLASS:** `MEASUREMENT_SCIENCE_LINEAGE`
- **INFORMATIONAL_RELATION:** `HYBRID_RESOLUTION`
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Use crossed/replicated reference and candidate observations to locate variation source before changing either side.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** A changed score alone cannot identify whether candidate, evaluator, fixture, environment or sample changed.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Fixing the wrong component, circular debugging and endless candidate/evaluator oscillation.
- **MECHANISM:** Maintain reference cases and stable candidates, cross evaluators/versions/operators, decompose variation and test specific mechanism hypotheses.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Unexpected regression/improvement, disagreement across evaluators, near-threshold failures or repeated nonreproducibility.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** If a direct inspection proves a deterministic candidate defect or evaluator bug, repair that source immediately and document it.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Stable reference/candidate artefacts, versioned evaluators, repeat/cross-over design and protected provenance.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Measurement runs are comparable; reference cases cover the disputed region.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Variance components/interactions are estimable enough to discriminate source or else conclusion remains unresolved.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Targets corrective work, preserves evidence and reduces blame/proxy mutation.
- **DECISION_OR_CONSUMER:** Engineer, measurement owner and independent review authority.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_OR_OVERREACTION_RISK:** Formal decomposition can be expensive and ambiguous when both candidate and evaluator adapt; variance attribution is not causal proof by itself.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_OR_MISSED_SIGNAL_RISK:** No separation makes every failure negotiable and every improvement suspect.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Only rerunning candidate; only checking easy references; evaluator and candidate changed together; no interaction analysis; regression to mean.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** The property is bounded by its stated assumptions and known failures: Only rerunning candidate; only checking easy references; evaluator and candidate changed together; no interaction analysis; regression to mean. Contrary evidence/limit: Variance decomposition can identify where variability resides but not automatically which construct is correct or why the interaction occurs.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Evolved from physical gauge R&R to general crossed evaluator/candidate/reference designs and configuration bridges.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Hold one side fixed while testing the other against independent references; leave unresolved when evidence cannot distinguish.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** A GR&R percentage is not required; discriminating source of variation is the property.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** `STRONGLY_RETAINED`
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** S058, S032, S024
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** S010, S052
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** S105
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE:** Variance decomposition can identify where variability resides but not automatically which construct is correct or why the interaction occurs.
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** How should source discrimination proceed when the evaluator is learned from candidate-generated data?

**EVIDENCE_STRENGTH**

| Partition | Judgement |
| --- | --- |
| HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| FORMAL_OR_THEORETICAL_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| MEASUREMENT_SCIENCE_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| EMPIRICAL_CAUSAL_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| EMPIRICAL_ASSOCIATIONAL_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| DOMAIN_CASE_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| REPLICATION_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY | HIGH |
| CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH | LOW |

**VARIATION_CONTROL_PROFILE**

- **APPLICABILITY:** NOT_PRIMARY_FOR_THIS_PROPERTY

**EXPERIMENT_DISCRIMINATION_PROFILE**

- **HYPOTHESIS_OR_DECISION:** Discriminate whether a changed result arises from candidate, evaluator or their interaction.
- **EXPERIMENTAL_UNIT:** Candidate/reference item crossed with evaluator/version/condition.
- **INDEPENDENCE_ASSUMPTION:** Repeated observations nested; independent items required for generalisation.
- **RANDOMISATION_OR_CONTROL:** Randomise/blind order and candidate identity.
- **BLOCKING_OR_STRATIFICATION:** Block time/environment and preserve bridge overlap.
- **REPLICATION_MEANING:** Multiple stable items and independently executed evaluator runs.
- **INTERACTION_RISK:** Candidate×evaluator interaction is often the decisive finding.
- **STOPPING_RULE:** Predefine suspected-source comparisons; do not stop at first favourable rerun.
- **EXPECTED_INFORMATION_GAIN:** Directs corrective action to the varying component.
- **COST:** Reference construction and crossed reruns.
- **FAILURE_IF_OVER_APPLIED:** Full crossing can be expensive or impossible for destructive systems.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Smallest crossed/bridge design that separates source, with unresolved outcome if not identifiable.

**QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT**

- Before changing the candidate, what evidence shows the evaluator is stable and valid?
- Before changing the evaluator, what evidence shows stable references are mismeasured?
- Were candidate and evaluator ever varied independently in a crossed design?

### P054 — Protect holdouts, reproduce analyses and prevent adaptive evaluation reuse from masquerading as fresh evidence

- **PROPERTY_ID:** `P054`
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Protect holdouts, reproduce analyses and prevent adaptive evaluation reuse from masquerading as fresh evidence
- **PROPERTY_FAMILY:** `PROXY_EVALUATOR_AND_SYSTEM_GOVERNANCE`
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Reproducible statistical analysis, multiple testing, reusable holdouts and modern benchmark-refresh evidence.
- **LINEAGE_CLASS:** `MODERN_DOMAIN_TRANSLATION`
- **INFORMATIONAL_RELATION:** `DOMAIN_TRANSLATION`
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Keep confirmation data/evaluators sufficiently independent of adaptive development and make the full analysis executable/reviewable.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Repeated tuning on a holdout leaks information; rerunning the same pipeline is reproducibility, not independent validation.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Benchmark saturation, test contamination, hidden analyst flexibility and false replication.
- **MECHANISM:** Separate development/exploration/confirmation, restrict feedback, log access, use reusable-holdout methods where applicable, refresh externally and reproduce exact computation.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Iterative software/ML/agent development, repeated benchmark submissions, many model/analysis variants or long campaigns.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For a deterministic exhaustive authoritative test, repeated execution may verify implementation but does not need a statistical holdout.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Versioned data/code/evaluator, access/provenance controls, representative fresh samples and a defined confirmation claim.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Evaluation pipeline itself is stable/validated; contamination/duplication detectable.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Protected sample remains sufficiently independent; reusable-holdout privacy/model conditions or fresh-data assumptions hold.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Preserves an honest out-of-sample error estimate and separates computational reproducibility from evidential independence.
- **DECISION_OR_CONSUMER:** Developer, benchmark owner, reviewer and deployment authority.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_OR_OVERREACTION_RISK:** Strict secrecy can reduce debugging/transparency; fresh sets may introduce shift and break comparability.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_OR_MISSED_SIGNAL_RISK:** Unprotected reuse makes apparent progress partly a property of evaluator exposure.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Calling same holdout reruns “replications”; public test labels; leaderboard tuning; analysis not executable; fresh test collected differently.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** The property is bounded by its stated assumptions and known failures: Calling same holdout reruns “replications”; public test labels; leaderboard tuning; analysis not executable; fresh test collected differently. Contrary evidence/limit: Fresh data can differ for reasons unrelated to overfitting; holdout protection trades off feedback, shift and sample efficiency.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Evolved from train/test splitting toward adaptive-data safeguards, access accounting, refreshed benchmarks and reproducibility packages.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** State what was reused, what was protected, and what evidence is genuinely fresh; use independent confirmation for final claims.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** A fixed split percentage or preregistration badge is not the property.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** `RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM`
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** S038, S039, S040
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** S033, S042
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** S037
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE:** Fresh data can differ for reasons unrelated to overfitting; holdout protection trades off feedback, shift and sample efficiency.
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** How can adaptive systems receive useful diagnostic feedback while preserving independent final evaluation?

**EVIDENCE_STRENGTH**

| Partition | Judgement |
| --- | --- |
| HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| FORMAL_OR_THEORETICAL_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| MEASUREMENT_SCIENCE_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| EMPIRICAL_CAUSAL_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| EMPIRICAL_ASSOCIATIONAL_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| DOMAIN_CASE_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| REPLICATION_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY | VERY_HIGH |
| CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH | HIGH |

**VARIATION_CONTROL_PROFILE**

- **APPLICABILITY:** NOT_PRIMARY_FOR_THIS_PROPERTY

**EXPERIMENT_DISCRIMINATION_PROFILE**

- **HYPOTHESIS_OR_DECISION:** Confirm generalisation after adaptive development/analysis.
- **EXPERIMENTAL_UNIT:** Fresh independently sampled evaluation unit.
- **INDEPENDENCE_ASSUMPTION:** Shared source/duplicates and repeated holdout use induce dependence.
- **RANDOMISATION_OR_CONTROL:** Treatment randomisation if causal; data access control for predictive confirmation.
- **BLOCKING_OR_STRATIFICATION:** Stratify fresh set by decision-relevant contexts.
- **REPLICATION_MEANING:** Independent fresh units/datasets, not resampling the exposed holdout.
- **INTERACTION_RISK:** Performance may interact with collection/context shift.
- **STOPPING_RULE:** Predefine final access/look or use valid reusable-holdout protocol.
- **EXPECTED_INFORMATION_GAIN:** Unbiased/controlled confirmation after adaptivity.
- **COST:** Fresh data, secrecy/access governance and reproducible pipeline.
- **FAILURE_IF_OVER_APPLIED:** Excessive holdout fragmentation or no debugging signal.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Transparent reuse accounting plus protected/fresh confirmation and shift analysis.

**QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT**

- Which results come from fresh protected evidence rather than evaluator reuse?
- Does exact computational reproduction add independent information or only verify execution?
- Has test-set access, contamination and feedback been audited?

### P055 — Check model adequacy and misspecification against the decision, not through ritual assumption tests

- **PROPERTY_ID:** `P055`
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Check model adequacy and misspecification against the decision, not through ritual assumption tests
- **PROPERTY_FAMILY:** `PROXY_EVALUATOR_AND_SYSTEM_GOVERNANCE`
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Residual diagnostics, Box model criticism, robust statistics, time-series/SPC and modern predictive checking.
- **LINEAGE_CLASS:** `REACTION_OR_CORRECTION`
- **INFORMATIONAL_RELATION:** `STATISTICAL_NATIVE`
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Ask whether model errors materially affect the action, using residual structure, calibration, predictive checks and sensitivity.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Mathematical validity under assumptions is mistaken for evidence those assumptions hold; normality-test rituals can reject harmless deviations or miss dependence.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Invalid intervals/control limits/extrapolations and false confidence in a convenient fitted model.
- **MECHANISM:** Inspect time/order/residual/influence/calibration plots, compare plausible models, simulate operating characteristics and use robust/design-based alternatives where needed.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Any inferential/control/reliability method whose operating characteristics depend materially on distribution, independence, link or functional form.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For deterministic checks or methods robust by design to the relevant deviation, skip irrelevant normality tests and document why.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Adequate data over intended region, stable measurement, model purpose and known decision-sensitive assumptions.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Measurement artefacts/censoring/rounding are modelled or ruled out before diagnosing process distribution.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Diagnostics have power for material deviations; alternative models are plausible and not chosen only for desired results.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Prevents assumption decorations from legitimising fragile decisions and focuses remediation on consequential departures.
- **DECISION_OR_CONSUMER:** Analyst, monitoring/experiment owner and reviewer.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_OR_OVERREACTION_RISK:** With small samples diagnostics have low power; with huge samples they detect trivial deviations; repeated model search adds multiplicity.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_OR_MISSED_SIGNAL_RISK:** No adequacy check can make nominal guarantees badly wrong.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Mandatory Shapiro–Wilk; accepting model because p>.05; residuals not time-ordered; transformation after seeing desired significance; no out-of-sample check.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** The property is bounded by its stated assumptions and known failures: Mandatory Shapiro–Wilk; accepting model because p>.05; residuals not time-ordered; transformation after seeing desired significance; no out-of-sample check. Contrary evidence/limit: Model adequacy is never globally established; it is conditional on data region and purpose, and diagnostics can share blind spots.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Evolved from pass/fail assumption tests to graphical/predictive/sensitivity assessment of decision consequences.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Use the simplest adequate model and report which guarantees are conditional; switch/robustify when a departure changes the decision.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** A normality test checklist is ceremony; decision-sensitive adequacy is the property.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** `ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE`
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** S017, S021, S110
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** S026, S031, S042
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** S099
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE:** Model adequacy is never globally established; it is conditional on data region and purpose, and diagnostics can share blind spots.
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** How should model uncertainty be communicated when several models are adequate centrally but diverge in the decision tail?

**EVIDENCE_STRENGTH**

| Partition | Judgement |
| --- | --- |
| HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| FORMAL_OR_THEORETICAL_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| MEASUREMENT_SCIENCE_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| EMPIRICAL_CAUSAL_STRENGTH | LOW |
| EMPIRICAL_ASSOCIATIONAL_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| DOMAIN_CASE_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| REPLICATION_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY | VERY_HIGH |
| CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH | HIGH |

**VARIATION_CONTROL_PROFILE**

- **DECISION_PROBLEM:** Validate the model used to set limits, detect shifts or predict capability.
- **ASSUMED_DATA_GENERATING_PROCESS:** Proposed probabilistic/dynamic model versus observed residual/predictive structure.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Stable measurements, time/order and enough baseline coverage.
- **SIGNAL_OR_DISCRIMINATOR:** Decision-relevant lack-of-fit, calibration or operating-characteristic failure.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_COST:** Discarding a useful simple model for trivial deviations.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_COST:** Using invalid limits/predictions under material misspecification.
- **NONSTATIONARITY_RISK:** Model adequacy can expire as process/context shifts.
- **INTERVENTION_RISK:** Refitting until alarms vanish can absorb real change.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use distribution-free/direct limits or mechanism-based discriminator.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Purpose-specific model checks, sensitivity and versioned refit governance.

**EXPERIMENT_DISCRIMINATION_PROFILE**

- **APPLICABILITY:** NOT_PRIMARY_FOR_THIS_PROPERTY

**QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT**

- Which assumption could materially change the decision?
- Were diagnostics matched to time, dependence, tails and prediction—not only normality?
- Is a robust or deterministic alternative available that avoids the fragile assumption?

### P056 — Integrate multiple statistical and engineering methods into a system-level evidence strategy

- **PROPERTY_ID:** `P056`
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Integrate multiple statistical and engineering methods into a system-level evidence strategy
- **PROPERTY_FAMILY:** `PROXY_EVALUATOR_AND_SYSTEM_GOVERNANCE`
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Quality planning/control traditions, Steiner–MacKay variation reduction and Hoerl–Snee/ISEA statistical engineering.
- **LINEAGE_CLASS:** `QUALITY_ENGINEERING_GENERALISATION`
- **INFORMATIONAL_RELATION:** `QUALITY_ANCESTRY`
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Structure large, complex, unstructured problems; sequence measurement, process, experiment, reliability and decision tools around a mission.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** A toolkit used piecemeal can optimise local metrics, repeat work, miss dependencies or apply the right method at the wrong time.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Cookbook statistics, tool proliferation, local optimisation and unresolved interfaces between measurement, experimentation and action.
- **MECHANISM:** Define mission/system, map evidence dependencies, identify dominant uncertainty, select/integrate the smallest valid methods, iterate with governance and sustain the solution.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Cross-disciplinary problems too large for one model/test and with multiple linked decisions.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For a well-structured local question solved by one authoritative method/check, use that method; system-level statistical engineering would be overhead.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Decision authority, cross-disciplinary domain knowledge, measurement integrity, provenance, resource model and explicit interfaces among methods.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Every input measurement/evaluator has sufficient fitness and common identities/units are maintained.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Integration logic is auditable; no method’s assumptions are silently contradicted by another stage; organisational cooperation exists.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Converts statistics from a menu into an evidence-efficient engineering system and improves sustainability/transfer of solutions.
- **DECISION_OR_CONSUMER:** System/problem owner, cross-functional engineering team and decision governance.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_OR_OVERREACTION_RISK:** The emerging discipline can become branding, consultant theatre or an unfalsifiable “use the right tools” slogan; causal programme evidence is limited.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_OR_MISSED_SIGNAL_RISK:** Single-tool thinking misses coupled measurement/process/decision failures.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Framework phases treated as deliverables; tool count mistaken for rigour; no dominant uncertainty; local Cpk improvement harms system outcome.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** The property is bounded by its stated assumptions and known failures: Framework phases treated as deliverables; tool count mistaken for rigour; no dominant uncertainty; local Cpk improvement harms system outcome. Contrary evidence/limit: Evidence is strong for component methods and plausible for integration, but weak for a unique discipline-wide causal effect independent of competent engineering management.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Broader statistical engineering generalises beyond a 2005 manufacturing algorithm and beyond Six Sigma, but remains an emerging discipline with case-heavy evidence.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** A problem-structuring and integration property: right evidence, right order, right scale, with assumptions and consumer decisions explicit.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** The label, framework diagram or consultancy is not the property; integration and sustainable decision improvement are.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** `RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM`
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** S079, S080, S081
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** S082, S084
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** S085, S086
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE:** Evidence is strong for component methods and plausible for integration, but weak for a unique discipline-wide causal effect independent of competent engineering management.
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Which integration patterns are distinctively statistical engineering rather than good systems engineering with statistics?

**EVIDENCE_STRENGTH**

| Partition | Judgement |
| --- | --- |
| HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| FORMAL_OR_THEORETICAL_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| MEASUREMENT_SCIENCE_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| EMPIRICAL_CAUSAL_STRENGTH | LOW |
| EMPIRICAL_ASSOCIATIONAL_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| DOMAIN_CASE_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| REPLICATION_STRENGTH | LOW |
| TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY | MODERATE |
| CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH | MODERATE |

**VARIATION_CONTROL_PROFILE**

- **APPLICABILITY:** NOT_PRIMARY_FOR_THIS_PROPERTY

**EXPERIMENT_DISCRIMINATION_PROFILE**

- **HYPOTHESIS_OR_DECISION:** Sequence a portfolio of experiments/measurements to resolve a complex system decision.
- **EXPERIMENTAL_UNIT:** Varies by subproblem; integration must preserve each true experimental unit.
- **INDEPENDENCE_ASSUMPTION:** Cross-study dependence/common data and shared evaluators must be represented.
- **RANDOMISATION_OR_CONTROL:** Use randomisation where causal; do not force it on deterministic subproblems.
- **BLOCKING_OR_STRATIFICATION:** Coordinate blocking/stratification across relevant interfaces.
- **REPLICATION_MEANING:** Independent confirmation at system-critical claims, not repeated local analyses.
- **INTERACTION_RISK:** Cross-component interactions and local-to-system trade-offs are central.
- **STOPPING_RULE:** Mission-level stopping based on remaining decision uncertainty/value.
- **EXPECTED_INFORMATION_GAIN:** Reduces dominant uncertainty across the whole problem.
- **COST:** Coordination, data integration and cross-disciplinary time.
- **FAILURE_IF_OVER_APPLIED:** Framework bureaucracy or endless decomposition.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Lean problem architecture with explicit evidence dependencies and local method cheap paths.

**QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT**

- Does the target problem require several evidence methods whose dependencies must be engineered?
- Is the dominant uncertainty identified before selecting tools?
- Could a single local method or deterministic check solve the decision more cheaply?

### P057 — Make measurement and controls economically proportional; retire, revalidate or recalibrate stale controls

- **PROPERTY_ID:** `P057`
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Make measurement and controls economically proportional; retire, revalidate or recalibrate stale controls
- **PROPERTY_FAMILY:** `PROXY_EVALUATOR_AND_SYSTEM_GOVERNANCE`
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Shewhart/Duncan economic control, value-of-information reasoning, calibration intervals and modern monitoring governance.
- **LINEAGE_CLASS:** `QUALITY_ENGINEERING_GENERALISATION`
- **INFORMATIONAL_RELATION:** `HYBRID_RESOLUTION`
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Maintain evidence only while it changes decisions at acceptable cost; reassess controls when process, risk, measurement or cheaper discriminators change.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Legacy dashboards/tests continue by habit, creating noise and cost; obsolete thresholds can govern a changed system.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Measurement debt, stale controls, alert fatigue, audit theatre and resources diverted from higher-value evidence.
- **MECHANISM:** Track each control’s decision consumer, failure mode, operating characteristics, cost and last validation; trigger retirement/recalibration when value or validity decays.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Long-lived quality systems, recurring tests, duplicated reviews, low-yield monitoring or changed process/evaluator context.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** Keep cheap high-value controls and legal/safety requirements even if failures are rare; deterministic checks may remain near-zero cost.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Control inventory/ownership, event/decision history, cost, process/evaluator version, replacement/retirement authority and risk constraints.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Control outcomes and false alarms/misses are measurable enough to assess utility; reference standards remain available.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Past yield predicts some future value or scenario analysis captures uncertainty; retirement does not violate unmodelled catastrophic safeguards.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Reduces evidence burden, prevents stale governance and reallocates effort to decisive measurements.
- **DECISION_OR_CONSUMER:** Control owner, risk authority, resource allocator and downstream consumer.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_OR_OVERREACTION_RISK:** Retiring a quiet control can remove defence against rare catastrophe; cost pressure can bias value estimates.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_OR_MISSED_SIGNAL_RISK:** Never reviewing controls accumulates brittle, gamed and contradictory gates.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Quota retained with no consumer; threshold never revalidated; duplicated tests treated as corroboration; control removed because it catches failures.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** The property is bounded by its stated assumptions and known failures: Quota retained with no consumer; threshold never revalidated; duplicated tests treated as corroboration; control removed because it catches failures. Contrary evidence/limit: Rare-event controls may have high option value despite little observed use; absence of catches is not sufficient retirement evidence.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Evolved from economic chart design to lifecycle governance across measurements, experiments, thresholds and benchmark controls.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Every recurring control has a current decision, validity evidence and proportional cost; otherwise simplify, replace, revalidate or retire with risk sign-off.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** A periodic review ceremony is not the property unless it can actually retire/change controls on evidence.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** `CONTEXT_DEPENDENT`
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** S002, S014, S098
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** S060, S084
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** S085
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE:** Rare-event controls may have high option value despite little observed use; absence of catches is not sufficient retirement evidence.
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** How can stale-control retirement be distinguished from gaming away inconvenient evidence?

**EVIDENCE_STRENGTH**

| Partition | Judgement |
| --- | --- |
| HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| FORMAL_OR_THEORETICAL_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| MEASUREMENT_SCIENCE_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| EMPIRICAL_CAUSAL_STRENGTH | LOW |
| EMPIRICAL_ASSOCIATIONAL_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| DOMAIN_CASE_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| REPLICATION_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY | HIGH |
| CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH | MODERATE |

**VARIATION_CONTROL_PROFILE**

- **DECISION_PROBLEM:** Retain, tune, replace or retire a recurring control.
- **ASSUMED_DATA_GENERATING_PROCESS:** Control outcomes and underlying risk/process evolve over time.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Usage/decision/false-alarm records and current reference validation.
- **SIGNAL_OR_DISCRIMINATOR:** Positive net decision value and valid calibration for present context.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_COST:** Ongoing cost, alert fatigue and tampering.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_COST:** Loss of early warning or rare-event defence.
- **NONSTATIONARITY_RISK:** Core: process/evaluator/risk drift makes controls stale.
- **INTERVENTION_RISK:** Retirement after inconvenient failures can be proxy gaming.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Retain a cheap deterministic check or consolidate duplicates.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Lifecycle evidence review with independent retirement authority and risk bounds.

**EXPERIMENT_DISCRIMINATION_PROFILE**

- **APPLICABILITY:** NOT_PRIMARY_FOR_THIS_PROPERTY

**QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT**

- What current decision does each recurring measurement/control change?
- When was its measurement validity and threshold last challenged against current context?
- Would retirement remove rare-event defence or merely ceremony?

### P058 — Universal p < 0.05 threshold as an engineering decision rule

- **PROPERTY_ID:** `P058`
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Universal p < 0.05 threshold as an engineering decision rule
- **PROPERTY_FAMILY:** `CEREMONY_REJECTION_OR_SUPERSESSION`
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Later textbook/journal/managerial simplification of significance testing, not a property asserted by Fisher or Neyman–Pearson as a universal engineering rule.
- **LINEAGE_CLASS:** `MANAGEMENT_PROGRAMME_TRANSLATION`
- **INFORMATIONAL_RELATION:** `ONLY_ANALOGOUS`
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Dichotomise results at a conventional significance threshold and treat crossing it as sufficient for action or truth.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Originally offered a convenient convention, but it fails to encode effect size, measurement validity, multiplicity, model adequacy or decision loss.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Bright-line publication/release decisions, p-hacking, trivial effects called important and non-significant results called equivalent.
- **MECHANISM:** No defensible general mechanism; replace with decision/effect/uncertainty design and use p-values only as one calibrated diagnostic where appropriate.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** No universal trigger. A predeclared alpha can be useful inside a specific confirmatory error-control design.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For deterministic checks, descriptive estimation, Bayesian/decision designs, equivalence or safety constraints, the convention may be irrelevant.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** If used: valid model/design, prespecified family/stopping, effect/margin and consequence-aware threshold.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Measurement and sampling validity must already hold.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** The test statistic/reference model and error family are correct; the chosen alpha reflects decision consequences—which 0.05 usually does not by default.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Within a valid design, a threshold can standardise one error rate; the universal convention offers no general engineering payoff.
- **DECISION_OR_CONSUMER:** Specific confirmatory protocol owner, not general management branding.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_OR_OVERREACTION_RISK:** False dichotomy, threshold gaming and loss of uncertainty information.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_OR_MISSED_SIGNAL_RISK:** Blanket rejection of p-values can also discard useful model/error diagnostics.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Optional stopping; unreported multiplicity; p=0.049 vs 0.051 treated qualitatively different; effect size absent.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** The property is bounded by its stated assumptions and known failures: Optional stopping; unreported multiplicity; p=0.049 vs 0.051 treated qualitatively different; effect size absent. Contrary evidence/limit: There remains legitimate disagreement over when significance language/thresholds are useful; the rejected claim is universality and sufficiency.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** ASA statements criticise misuse while the 2021 task force explicitly preserves properly applied p-values; the evidence rejects universal threshold ritual, not all tests.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Use error thresholds only when tied to a declared family, effect/decision and consequence; otherwise report continuous evidence and uncertainty.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** The universal rule is ceremony/rejected; calibrated hypothesis tests remain conditional tools.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** `REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED`
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** S033, S034, S035
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** S007, S042
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** S040
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE:** There remains legitimate disagreement over when significance language/thresholds are useful; the rejected claim is universality and sufficiency.
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Which engineering decision contexts genuinely benefit from a fixed error threshold rather than an explicit loss/interval rule?

**EVIDENCE_STRENGTH**

| Partition | Judgement |
| --- | --- |
| HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| FORMAL_OR_THEORETICAL_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| MEASUREMENT_SCIENCE_STRENGTH | LOW |
| EMPIRICAL_CAUSAL_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| EMPIRICAL_ASSOCIATIONAL_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| DOMAIN_CASE_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| REPLICATION_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY | VERY_HIGH |
| CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |

**VARIATION_CONTROL_PROFILE**

- **APPLICABILITY:** NOT_PRIMARY_FOR_THIS_PROPERTY

**EXPERIMENT_DISCRIMINATION_PROFILE**

- **HYPOTHESIS_OR_DECISION:** No universal hypothesis; valid only inside a specific confirmatory design.
- **EXPERIMENTAL_UNIT:** As defined by that design.
- **INDEPENDENCE_ASSUMPTION:** As required by the design.
- **RANDOMISATION_OR_CONTROL:** Randomisation/control may support the test but does not make 0.05 universal.
- **BLOCKING_OR_STRATIFICATION:** As needed.
- **REPLICATION_MEANING:** Independent units as designed.
- **INTERACTION_RISK:** Must be considered.
- **STOPPING_RULE:** Fixed/sequential rule must be explicit.
- **EXPECTED_INFORMATION_GAIN:** Only the design-specific error-control value.
- **COST:** May be low, but misuse cost high.
- **FAILURE_IF_OVER_APPLIED:** Threshold ritual substitutes convention for engineering consequence.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Conditional calibrated test subordinate to effect/uncertainty/decision.

**QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT**

- Is a conventional p-value cutoff being used as the entire decision rule?
- Where are effect magnitude, uncertainty, multiplicity and engineering consequence represented?
- Would an equivalence, interval, sequential or deterministic rule answer the decision better?

### P059 — Control-chart-everything and automatic run-rule escalation

- **PROPERTY_ID:** `P059`
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Control-chart-everything and automatic run-rule escalation
- **PROPERTY_FAMILY:** `CEREMONY_REJECTION_OR_SUPERSESSION`
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Textbook/quality-programme simplification of Shewhart SPC and later dashboard practice.
- **LINEAGE_CLASS:** `MANAGEMENT_PROGRAMME_TRANSLATION`
- **INFORMATIONAL_RELATION:** `ONLY_ANALOGOUS`
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Place every recurring metric on a classical chart and treat every rule violation as an intervention mandate.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Attempts to make variation visible, but assumes a meaningful stream, baseline, measurement and action response that often do not exist.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** False alarms, dashboard overload, worker surveillance/blame, invalid iid limits and tampering.
- **MECHANISM:** No universal mechanism; retain P015–P024 only where a calibrated monitor changes an actionable decision.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Only repeatable, adequately measured processes with an interpretable baseline and response plan.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** One-off heterogeneous work, exact deterministic checks, sparse changing mixtures or metrics without an owner should not trigger classical charting.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Stable measurement, process identity, dependence model, error costs and response capacity.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Metric must be decision-relevant and evaluator-stable.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Chart/model assumptions and baseline are adequate; alerts are investigated rather than punished.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Conditional charts can improve detection; universal chart deployment has no demonstrated general payoff.
- **DECISION_OR_CONSUMER:** Specific process owner, never a generic surveillance layer.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_OR_OVERREACTION_RISK:** Alert fatigue, false accusation and self-induced variation.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_OR_MISSED_SIGNAL_RISK:** Refusing all monitoring can miss gradual or recurrent change.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Specs used as limits; many supplementary rules; no action plan; rebaselining; individual ranking on system variation.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** The property is bounded by its stated assumptions and known failures: Specs used as limits; many supplementary rules; no action plan; rebaselining; individual ranking on system variation. Contrary evidence/limit: Some broad monitoring programmes work organisationally, but benefit cannot be inferred from chart count and may reflect improved attention/process definition.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** SPC literature itself documents assumptions and controversies; evolved practice narrows and adapts chart use.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Monitor only where the stream, signal, cost and response are defensible; otherwise choose a cheaper direct/qualitative method.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** The chart/tool is ceremony when detached from a decision; variation discrimination is the property.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** `CEREMONY_NOT_GENERAL_PROPERTY`
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** S001, S061, S107
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** S030, S031, S066
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** S032
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE:** Some broad monitoring programmes work organisationally, but benefit cannot be inferred from chart count and may reflect improved attention/process definition.
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** What minimum evidence shows a new chart adds discrimination rather than alert volume?

| MEASUREMENT_SCIENCE_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| EMPIRICAL_CAUSAL_STRENGTH | LOW |
| EMPIRICAL_ASSOCIATIONAL_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| DOMAIN_CASE_STRENGTH | HIGH |

**VARIATION_CONTROL_PROFILE**

- **DECISION_PROBLEM:** No universal decision; assess whether a proposed chart adds actionable detection.
- **ASSUMED_DATA_GENERATING_PROCESS:** Varies; often violated by heterogeneous/adaptive streams.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Must be established before use.
- **SIGNAL_OR_DISCRIMINATOR:** Incremental detection value over cheap baseline.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_COST:** Dashboard overload, blame and tampering.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_COST:** Missed change if all monitoring rejected.
- **NONSTATIONARITY_RISK:** Often high.
- **INTERVENTION_RISK:** Automatic intervention is central hazard.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Direct check, simple plot or case review.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Only retain calibrated, owned monitors with positive decision value.

**EXPERIMENT_DISCRIMINATION_PROFILE**

- **APPLICABILITY:** NOT_PRIMARY_FOR_THIS_PROPERTY

**QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT**

- Does each chart have a defined decision, validated baseline and response owner?
- Are run-rule false-alarm costs and dependence accounted for?
- Would a direct deterministic check or simple time plot suffice?

### P060 — Universal 3.4 DPMO or “six-sigma level” as the definition of quality

- **PROPERTY_ID:** `P060`
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Universal 3.4 DPMO or “six-sigma level” as the definition of quality
- **PROPERTY_FAMILY:** `CEREMONY_REJECTION_OR_SUPERSESSION`
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Motorola/GE-era Six Sigma rhetoric and capability-index conversion conventions, including the contested 1.5-sigma shift.
- **LINEAGE_CLASS:** `MANAGEMENT_PROGRAMME_TRANSLATION`
- **INFORMATIONAL_RELATION:** `MANAGEMENT_PROGRAMME_TRANSLATION`
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Express quality as a universal defects-per-million target associated with a nominal shifted normal process.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Provides a memorable aspiration but compresses defect opportunity, severity, dependence, distribution, stability and measurement into one number.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** False precision, opportunity-count gaming, normal-tail extrapolation, equal treatment of trivial/catastrophic defects and branding substitution.
- **MECHANISM:** No general mechanism; use direct consequence-specific defect/reliability/tail evidence and conditional capability only where prerequisites hold.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** A project may choose a defect-rate target when opportunities and consequences are coherent; the branded universal number is not required.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** Do not trigger for continuous performance, changing opportunities, unstable/non-normal processes, rare catastrophic modes or deterministic requirements.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Stable process, valid measurement, fixed opportunity definition, adequate distribution/tail evidence and consequence-relevant target.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Defect classification and opportunity denominators must be stable and resistant to gaming.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Normal/shift model and independence would need support; extrapolation far beyond data remains weak.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** A context-specific defect goal can focus effort; universal 3.4 DPMO has no general engineering validity.
- **DECISION_OR_CONSUMER:** Programme/project owner only after risk justification.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_OR_OVERREACTION_RISK:** Drives denominator relabelling and unsafe equivalence of defects.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_OR_MISSED_SIGNAL_RISK:** Rejecting all defect-rate goals can lose a useful operational metric.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Counting opportunities strategically; Cpk converted to DPMO under instability; 1.5 shift asserted as law; average defect severity ignored.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** The property is bounded by its stated assumptions and known failures: Counting opportunities strategically; Cpk converted to DPMO under instability; 1.5 shift asserted as law; average defect severity ignored. Contrary evidence/limit: Historical details of the 1.5-sigma convention vary across practitioner accounts; no strong general evidence makes it a physical/statistical law.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Mature quality engineering retains conditional capability/yield and abandons the number as a universal definition.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Set targets from customer/safety/economic consequences and observed/modelled risk, not brand numerology.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** 3.4 DPMO and “sigma level” are programme rhetoric/conditional conversion, not a surviving general property.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** `CEREMONY_NOT_GENERAL_PROPERTY`
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** S082, S062, S063
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** S084, S031, S048
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** S085, S086
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE:** Historical details of the 1.5-sigma convention vary across practitioner accounts; no strong general evidence makes it a physical/statistical law.
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** In which narrowly defined high-volume processes is a DPMO target more decision-useful than direct yield/severity/tail reporting?

- Is the DPMO/sigma claim based on a stable measured process and defensible opportunity count?
- Are defect severities and tail/model uncertainty hidden by one number?
- Was the target derived from consequences or inherited from branding?

### P061 — DMAIC/DMADV phase names and tollgates as a general statistical property

- **PROPERTY_ID:** `P061`
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** DMAIC/DMADV phase names and tollgates as a general statistical property
- **PROPERTY_FAMILY:** `CEREMONY_REJECTION_OR_SUPERSESSION`
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Six Sigma management-programme translation of earlier problem-solving, quality-control and product-design cycles.
- **LINEAGE_CLASS:** `MANAGEMENT_PROGRAMME_TRANSLATION`
- **INFORMATIONAL_RELATION:** `MANAGEMENT_PROGRAMME_TRANSLATION`
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Require work to pass named phases and prescribed tollgates/templates.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Can coordinate roles, preserve sequencing and prevent premature solutioning in large programmes.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Phase compliance replacing evidence, forced linearity, duplicated paperwork and tools chosen to satisfy a gate.
- **MECHANISM:** Underlying properties—problem definition, measurement validation, analysis, intervention, confirmation/control—can be implemented in many forms.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Useful when many teams/projects need common governance and handoffs.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** Small/local/deterministic decisions, exploratory science, or established competent workflows need not adopt the names or fixed order.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Real authority at gates, evidence criteria, proportionate templates and ability to iterate/skip non-value steps.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Measurement validity cannot be conferred by reaching the Measure phase.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Programme fit and organisational coordination are adequate; phases permit iteration and exceptions.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** May improve coordination/discipline, but names themselves do not generate valid evidence.
- **DECISION_OR_CONSUMER:** Programme/project governance.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_OR_OVERREACTION_RISK:** Bureaucracy, delay and false completion.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_OR_MISSED_SIGNAL_RISK:** No structure can permit premature action or lost evidence in complex programmes.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Tollgate passed on documents; analysis forced after answer known; no iteration; every project uses same tool menu.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** The property is bounded by its stated assumptions and known failures: Tollgate passed on documents; analysis forced after answer known; no iteration; every project uses same tool menu. Contrary evidence/limit: Observational evidence cannot cleanly separate structured problem solving from brand, training, leadership and selection effects.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Research characterises DMAIC as a problem-solving structure and Six Sigma’s distinctive element as organisational architecture, not unique statistics.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Retain evidence dependencies and governance where useful; strip mandatory branding and tailor path to problem risk/uncertainty.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Phase names/tollgates are artefacts, not general properties.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** `CEREMONY_NOT_GENERAL_PROPERTY`
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** S082, S083, S091
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** S084, S087
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** S085, S086
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE:** Observational evidence cannot cleanly separate structured problem solving from brand, training, leadership and selection effects.
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Which coordination benefits persist after removing DMAIC names and mandatory artefacts?

### P062 — Belt hierarchy and certification as a general engineering property

- **PROPERTY_ID:** `P062`
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Belt hierarchy and certification as a general engineering property
- **PROPERTY_FAMILY:** `CEREMONY_REJECTION_OR_SUPERSESSION`
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Six Sigma organisational role structure and certification market.
- **LINEAGE_CLASS:** `MANAGEMENT_PROGRAMME_TRANSLATION`
- **INFORMATIONAL_RELATION:** `MANAGEMENT_PROGRAMME_TRANSLATION`
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Assign improvement authority/competence through belt ranks and certified tool curricula.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Can create trained specialists, common language, mentoring and protected project capacity.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Credential theatre, consultant dependence, fixed tool menus and authority detached from domain/measurement competence.
- **MECHANISM:** Underlying properties are demonstrated competence, role clarity, independence and capacity—not belt names.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Potentially useful in large deployments needing specialist career/role architecture.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** Small teams, mature statistical functions, regulated laboratories or domain specialists may use different competence systems.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Validated competence assessment, domain integration, project evidence, role authority and ongoing learning.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Certification must include measurement/design reasoning, not merely software/tool recall.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Curriculum/assessment predicts competent practice and role structure fits the organisation.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** May improve capability and coordination; certification alone has weak causal evidence.
- **DECISION_OR_CONSUMER:** Organisational leadership and project portfolio governance.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_OR_OVERREACTION_RISK:** Hierarchy suppresses local expertise, encourages template compliance and creates commercial incentives.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_OR_MISSED_SIGNAL_RISK:** No specialist capacity can leave complex statistical work unsupported.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Certificate accepted as evidence of method validity; belt owns statistics without process knowledge; project quotas; expired skills.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** The property is bounded by its stated assumptions and known failures: Certificate accepted as evidence of method validity; belt owns statistics without process knowledge; project quotas; expired skills. Contrary evidence/limit: Some evidence links experienced teams/structured roles to project success, but credential effects are confounded with selection, resources and leadership.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Organisational research treats role structure as distinctive to Six Sigma, while criticism shows resource/rigidity/theatre risks.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Use role/competence systems proportionate to need and assess actual decisions/work products; no required belt taxonomy.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Belt titles/certificates are programme artefacts.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** `CEREMONY_NOT_GENERAL_PROPERTY`
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** S082, S085, S086
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** S084, S087
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** S085
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE:** Some evidence links experienced teams/structured roles to project success, but credential effects are confounded with selection, resources and leadership.
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** What competence evidence predicts high-quality statistical engineering better than course completion?

### P063 — More samples, more reruns or repeated green tests automatically mean more truth

- **PROPERTY_ID:** `P063`
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** More samples, more reruns or repeated green tests automatically mean more truth
- **PROPERTY_FAMILY:** `CEREMONY_REJECTION_OR_SUPERSESSION`
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** A recurring statistical caricature contradicted by sampling, measurement error, dependence, missingness, sequential and adaptive-data theory.
- **LINEAGE_CLASS:** `REACTION_OR_CORRECTION`
- **INFORMATIONAL_RELATION:** `CONVERGENT_PROPERTY`
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Treat raw observation count or repeated pass count as monotonically increasing confidence regardless of source/dependence/bias.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Averaging can reduce independent random error, but cannot remove shared bias, selection, invalid constructs or common-mode dependence.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** False precision, pseudo-replication, confirmation loops and quota-based evidence theatre.
- **MECHANISM:** Count independent information, model dependence/bias, protect stopping and ask whether the next observation can change the decision.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** No universal trigger; more evidence helps only when it is valid, sufficiently independent and decision-relevant.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** Stop when deterministic evidence settles the decision or when another correlated rerun has negligible value.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Valid measurement, representative sampling, independent/new units or valid dependence/sequential model.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Measurement bias and evaluator drift must be bounded; repeated runs are provenance-distinct.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Information increments satisfy the planning model and no outcome-contingent selection/stopping invalidates inference.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Valid additional units improve precision/power; the universal claim is rejected.
- **DECISION_OR_CONSUMER:** Experiment/measurement owner.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_OR_OVERREACTION_RISK:** Endless data collection, overpowered triviality and cost/harm.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_OR_MISSED_SIGNAL_RISK:** Stopping too early can leave a consequential effect unresolved.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Same data reanalysed; same unit reread; same seed; same evaluator bias; stop at green; failed runs dropped.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** The property is bounded by its stated assumptions and known failures: Same data reanalysed; same unit reread; same seed; same evaluator bias; stop at green; failed runs dropped. Contrary evidence/limit: Information dependence can be only partly known; conservative effective sample sizes or fresh-source confirmation may be required.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Modern practice distinguishes technical repeatability, independent replication, effective sample size, sequential validity and value of information.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Another observation earns weight only through identifiable independent/conditional information and positive decision value.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Raw run count and streaks are not properties.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** `REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED`
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** S023, S028, S032, S038
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** S040, S042
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** S039
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE:** Information dependence can be only partly known; conservative effective sample sizes or fresh-source confirmation may be required.
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** How should shared upstream training/data/evaluator dependence be discounted when nominal runs are distinct?

**EVIDENCE_STRENGTH**

| Partition | Judgement |
| --- | --- |
| HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| FORMAL_OR_THEORETICAL_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| MEASUREMENT_SCIENCE_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| EMPIRICAL_CAUSAL_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| EMPIRICAL_ASSOCIATIONAL_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| DOMAIN_CASE_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| REPLICATION_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
| ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY | VERY_HIGH |
| CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH | LOW |

**VARIATION_CONTROL_PROFILE**

- **APPLICABILITY:** NOT_PRIMARY_FOR_THIS_PROPERTY

**EXPERIMENT_DISCRIMINATION_PROFILE**

- **HYPOTHESIS_OR_DECISION:** No universal hypothesis; assess incremental information of repeated testing.
- **EXPERIMENTAL_UNIT:** True independent unit, not each output/review.
- **INDEPENDENCE_ASSUMPTION:** Central: correlation/common source must be modelled.
- **RANDOMISATION_OR_CONTROL:** As needed for causal questions.
- **BLOCKING_OR_STRATIFICATION:** Block shared sources rather than count them as replication.
- **REPLICATION_MEANING:** New independent units or valid sequential information increments.
- **INTERACTION_RISK:** Shared evaluator/candidate interactions can make streaks misleading.
- **STOPPING_RULE:** Fixed/valid sequential/value-based stop.
- **EXPECTED_INFORMATION_GAIN:** Positive only when uncertainty relevant to action decreases.
- **COST:** Potentially unbounded waste.
- **FAILURE_IF_OVER_APPLIED:** Quotas and streak worship.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Effective-information accounting plus stopping/value boundary.

**QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT**

- What new independent information does the next run add?
- Are repeated green results sharing the same measurement bias, source or selection process?
- Has the decision already been discriminated by cheaper authoritative evidence?

### P064 — Mandatory normality tests, fixed gauge R&R cutoffs and full MSA paperwork for every metric

- **PROPERTY_ID:** `P064`
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Mandatory normality tests, fixed gauge R&R cutoffs and full MSA paperwork for every metric
- **PROPERTY_FAMILY:** `CEREMONY_REJECTION_OR_SUPERSESSION`
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Quality-training/checklist simplification of model diagnostics and measurement-system analysis.
- **LINEAGE_CLASS:** `MANAGEMENT_PROGRAMME_TRANSLATION`
- **INFORMATIONAL_RELATION:** `ONLY_ANALOGOUS`
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Require standard assumption tests and form thresholds regardless of decision, scale, sample size or measurement mechanism.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Attempts to standardise due diligence but confuses a procedure with evidence of adequacy.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Small-sample non-detection, huge-sample trivial rejection, arbitrary GR&R pass/fail and paperwork without construct validity.
- **MECHANISM:** Replace with decision-sensitive measurement capability, graphical/predictive diagnostics and methods robust to relevant departures.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Use formal tests/designs when a specific assumption or variance component can change the decision.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** Skip irrelevant tests for deterministic checks, categorical authoritative outcomes or robust methods; use a concise rationale.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Defined decision margin, measurement mechanism, model purpose and representative validation conditions.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Measurement study must cover actual range/operators/context rather than satisfy a form.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Diagnostic/test operating characteristics and cutoff relevance are understood.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Targeted checks can prevent invalid inference; mandatory ritual has no general payoff.
- **DECISION_OR_CONSUMER:** Measurement/analysis owner and reviewer.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_OR_OVERREACTION_RISK:** Unnecessary rejection, delay and false sense of compliance.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_OR_MISSED_SIGNAL_RISK:** Omitting all diagnostics can miss consequential defects.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** p>0.05 called normal; 10% GR&R used universally; easy part range; exact digital check forced into variance study.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** The property is bounded by its stated assumptions and known failures: p>0.05 called normal; 10% GR&R used universally; easy part range; exact digital check forced into variance study. Contrary evidence/limit: Some regulated contexts legitimately require standardised evidence for comparability; the critique is indiscriminate use, not all standard protocols.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Mature practice evaluates fitness relative to use and consequential departures, not universal checklist thresholds.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Use the smallest diagnostic/MSA design that can falsify decision adequacy.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** The mandatory form/cutoff is ceremony; measurement fitness/model adequacy are retained properties.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** `CEREMONY_NOT_GENERAL_PROPERTY`
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** S055, S058, S110
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** S032, S042
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** S105
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE:** Some regulated contexts legitimately require standardised evidence for comparability; the critique is indiscriminate use, not all standard protocols.
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Which standardised diagnostics provide enough cross-site governance value to justify occasional local over-testing?

**EVIDENCE_STRENGTH**

| Partition | Judgement |
| --- | --- |
| HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| FORMAL_OR_THEORETICAL_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| MEASUREMENT_SCIENCE_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| EMPIRICAL_CAUSAL_STRENGTH | LOW |
| EMPIRICAL_ASSOCIATIONAL_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| DOMAIN_CASE_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| REPLICATION_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY | VERY_HIGH |
| CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH | HIGH |

**VARIATION_CONTROL_PROFILE**

- **APPLICABILITY:** NOT_PRIMARY_FOR_THIS_PROPERTY

**EXPERIMENT_DISCRIMINATION_PROFILE**

- **APPLICABILITY:** NOT_PRIMARY_FOR_THIS_PROPERTY

**QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT**

- What decision would the normality test or GR&R cutoff change?
- Does the study span the real decision range and variation sources?
- Would a robust method or direct reference comparison be cheaper and stronger?

### P065 — Universal Taguchi signal-to-noise ratios and orthogonal-array recipes as the definition of robust design

- **PROPERTY_ID:** `P065`
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Universal Taguchi signal-to-noise ratios and orthogonal-array recipes as the definition of robust design
- **PROPERTY_FAMILY:** `CEREMONY_REJECTION_OR_SUPERSESSION`
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Taguchi quality-engineering programme and later statistical critique/refinement.
- **LINEAGE_CLASS:** `RELIABILITY_OR_ROBUSTNESS_LINEAGE`
- **INFORMATIONAL_RELATION:** `RELIABILITY_OR_ROBUSTNESS_LINEAGE`
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Use prescribed S/N criteria and orthogonal arrays as generally optimal robust-design analysis.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Promoted explicit noise-factor design and off-line quality improvement but often imposed inefficient or condition-specific analyses.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Loss of information, confounded effects, wrong mean–variance criterion and ritual array selection.
- **MECHANISM:** Retain P043 robust/noise-factor experimentation; use standard factorial/RSM/direct mean–variance/tail models suited to the objective.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Particular S/N ratios may be useful only when their mean-variance/performance relation matches the engineering loss.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** Do not trigger automatically because a problem mentions robustness or noise.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Explicit loss/objective, factor/noise structure, alias analysis, measurement fitness and confirmation.
- **MEASUREMENT_PRECONDITIONS:** Response scale and variance can be estimated separately across representative noise conditions.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Chosen criterion has a justified relation to adjustment, mean and variance; array resolution supports interactions of interest.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Robust-design philosophy can be valuable; universal recipe is superseded.
- **DECISION_OR_CONSUMER:** Design/quality engineer.
- **FALSE_POSITIVE_OR_OVERREACTION_RISK:** Discarded information, false optimum and mystique around proprietary/brand rules.
- **FALSE_NEGATIVE_OR_MISSED_SIGNAL_RISK:** Rejecting Taguchi wholesale can lose the important design-for-insensitivity contribution.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** S/N used without loss relation; no interaction/alias analysis; array selected from menu; confirmation absent.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** The property is bounded by its stated assumptions and known failures: S/N used without loss relation; no interaction/alias analysis; array selected from menu; confirmation absent. Contrary evidence/limit: Taguchi’s organisational influence and practical success cases are real but do not establish statistical optimality of every prescribed method.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Box/Bisgaard/Fung, León/Shoemaker/Kackar, Nair panel and later practice retained robust parameter design while narrowing/replacing universal S/N methods.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Model the actual mean, variance, tails and noise-factor interactions; use a Taguchi criterion only when demonstrably appropriate.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Universal S/N/array recipe is superseded; robust design remains retained in evolved form.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** `SUPERSEDED_BY_STRONGER_FORM`
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** S094, S095, S096
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** S025, S102
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** S049, S050
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE:** Taguchi’s organisational influence and practical success cases are real but do not establish statistical optimality of every prescribed method.
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Which Taguchi-specific criteria remain preferable under clearly stated loss/mean-variance structures?

| --- | --- |

| FORMAL_OR_THEORETICAL_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| MEASUREMENT_SCIENCE_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| EMPIRICAL_CAUSAL_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| EMPIRICAL_ASSOCIATIONAL_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| DOMAIN_CASE_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| REPLICATION_STRENGTH | HIGH |
| TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH | MODERATE |
| ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY | HIGH |
| CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH | VERY_HIGH |
